<a id="r21"></a>
### R21. Total-variation/overlap identity and classical Fuchs–van de Graaf bounds for finite distributions

- **Statement.** For P0, P1 : ℕ → ℕ with equal totals D: Σ|P0−P1| + 2·Σmin(P0,P1) = 2D, with three corollaries. For r, s : ℕ → ℕ with Σr² = Σs² = M and B = Σ r·s: overlap ≤ B ≤ M, 2M ≤ TV₂ + 2B and TV₂² + 4B² ≤ 4M²; and |⟨u,v⟩|² ≤ M² for complex integer vectors of squared norm M.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`SecMeshPQSI.Solution.t7b_hiding_binding_conservation_nogo`](https://github.com/0thernes/secmesh-pqsi/blob/6c7f6421465c0018f2cfa385ede6f1041a62e543/lean/Solution.lean#L104), [`SecMeshPQSI.Solution.t7c_fuchs_vandegraaff_microcrypt_efi`](https://github.com/0thernes/secmesh-pqsi/blob/6c7f6421465c0018f2cfa385ede6f1041a62e543/lean/Solution.lean#L107); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** No commitment protocol is formalized; bit-commitment impossibility and 'architectural necessity' are informal readings.
- **Limit.** Only the classical case with perfect-square integer weights is covered; quantum Fuchs–van de Graaf for density operators is not.
- **Limit.** The pure-state relation D_tr² + F = 1 holds by the definition of D_tr² in Lean.
- **Limit.** No EFI pair or computational indistinguishability is defined.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin (latest release `v3.0.1`). They are not covered by this audit.

### R22. Polynomial identities for a one-parameter two-qubit family

- **Statement.** For integers with ca² + sa² = R²: (sa²−ca²)² + (2·ca·sa)² = R⁴ (read on paper as c_eff² + C² = 1); with also ch² + sh² = R², a scaled identity the paper reads as det(g) − Ω²/4 = (C²/4)·sin²(θ/2); and Cauchy–Schwarz-type bounds for sums over Gaussian-integer vectors.

Interpretation as Berry flux, concurrence, quantum geometric tensor and min-entropy is paper-level and numerical.

- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin (latest release `v3.0.1`). They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="r24"></a>
### R24. Moment-vector annihilation and a Pascal-type binomial identity used in a coded-PIR construction

- **Statement.** Over any commutative ring, the inner product of the moment vector (1,a,...,a^{T-1}) with e_{r+1} - a e_r is 0 for r+1<T, and K*C(T,T') = (K+T-T')*C(T-1,T'-1) + (K-T')*C(T-1,T') for 1<=T'<=min(T,K); plus closed arithmetic at T=K=3: with L(T') = (6-T')*C(2,T'-1), 3L(1)+3L(2)+L(3) = 42 and 60/(60+42) = 10/17 > 4/7.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declaration [`TriadFrontier.PIRKoszul.t1a_koszul_veronese_rank_identity`](https://github.com/0thernes/coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab/blob/e13761c9334da43ac160cbe0f0e14013bbd87ff1/lean/TriadFrontier/PIRKoszul.lean#L79); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. In v5.2.0, Lean T1k (PIRProtocol.lean) proves field-universal 2-file N-server K-MDS flag-private PIR exact zero-error decoding and two-sided colluder-transcript bijection Psi_I, instantiated in Python and ISO C11 by an explicit F_101 (6,3,3) rate-10/17 witness verified across all 20/20 colluding triples and 10/10 live encode/decode trials; the general min-formula remains outside Lean.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** Not shown in Lean: the general Schur-product min-formula or optimality over arbitrary query designs; v5.2.0 adds Lean T1k (field-universal exact decoding & bijection Psi_I) plus an explicit F_101 20/20 (6,3,3) witness in Python and C11 (closing ERRATA S01/A02).
- **Limit.** The parent Sun-Tao-Xu-Zhang maximum-redundancy and zero-error T>=3 questions remain open.
- **Limit.** The hub claim that this resolves a published question is not supported.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`
- **Source.** [coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab](https://github.com/0thernes/coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab) `v5.3.0` · archive entry `0TH-2026-012` · [paper](papers/0TH-2026-012_coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab/Donahue_2026_coded_pir_mlwe_complexity_frontier.pdf)

<a id="r25"></a>
### R25. Ring-universal vanishing of DFT coefficients for anti-periodic and block-twisted sequences

- **Statement.** Over any commutative ring: if ω^D = -1 and f(D+x) = -f(x) for x < D, every even-index coefficient of the length-2D DFT of f vanishes; if ω^D = ζ, f(qD+x) = ζ^{qr} f(x) for q < m and x < D, and Σ_{q<m} ζ^{q(r+k)} = 0, the k-th length-mD DFT coefficient vanishes. For odd c and D, c(x+D)^2 = cx^2 + D·c(2x+D) with c(2x+D) odd.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`TriadFrontier.MLWETightness.t2b_chen_step9_antiperiodic_dft_cancellation`](https://github.com/0thernes/coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab/blob/e13761c9334da43ac160cbe0f0e14013bbd87ff1/lean/TriadFrontier/MLWETightness.lean#L110), [`TriadFrontier.MLWETightness.t2f_universal_twisted_dft_selection_rule`](https://github.com/0thernes/coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab/blob/e13761c9334da43ac160cbe0f0e14013bbd87ff1/lean/TriadFrontier/MLWETightness.lean#L663); axioms: propext, Quot.sound (T2b); propext (T2f). All Renyi divergences, coupled FIPS 203 DFRs (cross-verified in v5.2.0 by a 90-digit Kronecker exact interval oracle matching NIST Table 1 within 0.04 bits, closing ERRATA S02), and Bai-overhead benchmarks are evaluated outside Lean; Lean certifies the integer moment and Renyi-2 DPI/tensorization inequalities.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** The earlier T2a 'refutation of Luo' and Gaussian DFR 'paradox' prose claims were withdrawn in v5.1.0 (ERRATA A04, A07), Hongxun Wu and Thomas Vidick are credited for finding the Chen Step-9 bug (ERRATA A06, L04), and v5.2.0 fixes the coupled FIPS 203 DFR convolution with 90-digit exact interval certification (ERRATA S02).
- **Limit.** The integer Cauchy-Schwarz, Holder, data-processing and q^m determinant lemmas in the same file (t2a, t2c-t2i) are not cited by this record and are not covered by it.
- **Limit.** Lattice hardness of MLWE is untouched.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`
- **Source.** [coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab](https://github.com/0thernes/coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab) `v5.3.0` · archive entry `0TH-2026-012` · [paper](papers/0TH-2026-012_coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab/Donahue_2026_coded_pir_mlwe_complexity_frontier.pdf)

<a id="r26"></a>
### R26. Characteristic-free formalizations of matrix-multiplication tensor rank, Schwartz-Zippel, and Shannon-capacity bounds for conference graphs

- **Statement.** Over every field: the 2x2 matrix multiplication tensor has rank exactly 7; rank(<m,m,m>) >= 2m^2-m and the two-sided bound 2*4^k-2^k <= rank(<2^k,2^k,2^k>) <= 7^k; a Schwartz-Zippel root bound; Theta(C5)=sqrt(5) in integer form; and, for every self-complementary conference graph on 4k+1 vertices, alpha(G^t)^2 <= (4k+1)^t with alpha(G^{2m}) >= (4k+1)^m (instantiated for C5, P13, P17).
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`TriadFrontier.StrassenWinograd.t3h_strassen_winograd_exact_rank_seven`](https://github.com/0thernes/coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab/blob/e13761c9334da43ac160cbe0f0e14013bbd87ff1/lean/TriadFrontier/StrassenWinograd.lean#L1558), [`TriadFrontier.BrockettDobkin.t3i_brockett_dobkin_rectangular_and_quadratic_matmul_bound`](https://github.com/0thernes/coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab/blob/e13761c9334da43ac160cbe0f0e14013bbd87ff1/lean/TriadFrontier/BrockettDobkin.lean#L701), [`TriadFrontier.ShannonCapacity.t3j_shannon_capacity_lovasz_c5_and_haemers`](https://github.com/0thernes/coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab/blob/e13761c9334da43ac160cbe0f0e14013bbd87ff1/lean/TriadFrontier/ShannonCapacity.lean#L1716), [`TriadFrontier.AlgebraicComplexity.t3k_schwartz_zippel_and_strassen_sandwich`](https://github.com/0thernes/coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab/blob/e13761c9334da43ac160cbe0f0e14013bbd87ff1/lean/TriadFrontier/AlgebraicComplexity.lean#L937), [`TriadFrontier.PaleyCapacity.t3l_infinite_paley_conference_shannon_capacity`](https://github.com/0thernes/coded-pir-mlwe-complexity-lab/blob/e13761c9334da43ac160cbe0f0e14013bbd87ff1/lean/TriadFrontier/PaleyCapacity.lean#L1909); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** T3i's 2m^2-m is weaker than the published Brockett-Dobkin bound 2n^2-1 (Linear Algebra Appl. 1978, doi:10.1016/0024-3795(78)90012-5); the 'sandwich' is two one-sided bounds, not an exact value.
- **Limit.** T3l's general clauses are conditional on the conference-graph and self-complementing-map hypotheses, which are verified only for C5 (in T3j), P13 and P17; infinitely many Paley graphs are not constructed. Theta = sqrt(n) for vertex-transitive self-complementary graphs is Lovász (1979, doi:10.1109/TIT.1979.1055985).
- **Limit.** omega=2 and the exact Theta(C7) stay open.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`

<a id="r27"></a>
### R27. Baker-Gill-Solovay oracle theorem in an explicit oracle Turing machine model

- **Statement.** In a 4-tape oracle Turing machine model with time bounds (n+1)^c + c: for every oracle O, P^O ⊆ NP^O; there is an oracle A with P^A = NP^A; there is an oracle B and a language L with L ∈ NP^B and L ∉ P^B. Oracles are arbitrary functions from bit strings to Booleans.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Solution.prove_p_subset_np`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/Solution.lean#L19), [`Solution.prove_baker_gill_solovay`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/Solution.lean#L22), [`PVsNP.p_subset_np`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/OTM.lean#L941), [`PVsNP.baker_gill_solovay`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/Relativization.lean#L20), [`PVsNP.bgs_oracle_collapse`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/RelativizationCol.lean#L1763), [`PVsNP.bgs_oracle_separation`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/RelativizationSep.lean#L591); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Six kernel checks seen in Ubuntu CI log only; not re-run on this machine.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** Holds for this machine model only; equivalence with standard Turing machine models is not formalized.
- **Limit.** Docstrings call the oracles computable; computability is not part of the statement.
- **Limit.** Says nothing about unrelativized P vs NP.
- **Limit.** Earlier machine formalizations of this theorem were not searched for.

<a id="r28"></a>
### R28. Counting lemma underlying the natural-proofs argument

- **Statement.** For fixed n and k: if a Boolean property accepts at least numer/denom of all 2^(2^n) truth tables and rejects every member of a set F, and a keyed family maps every k-bit key into F, then the property accepts no key and its acceptance gap is at least numer/denom. Also: an integer telescoping pigeonhole bound, and 'non-constant' accepts at least half of all tables for n ≥ 1.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Solution.prove_natural_proof_breaks_prf`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/Solution.lean#L25), [`PVsNP.natural_proof_breaks_prf`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/NaturalProofs.lean#L97), [`PVsNP.hybrid_telescoping_bound`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/NaturalProofs.lean#L132), [`PVsNP.natural_proof_nonvacuous`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/NaturalProofs.lean#L321); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound (C3, C5); propext, Quot.sound (C4).
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** Not the Razborov-Rudich theorem: constructivity, a circuit class, pseudorandomness and asymptotics are absent.
- **Limit.** The property is an arbitrary function, so the 'distinguisher' has no efficiency bound.
- **Limit.** The telescoping lemma is the pigeonhole corollary, not the summed triangle inequality stated in the paper.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`

<a id="r29"></a>
### R29. Minimally unsatisfiable parity systems and Boolean polymorphism facts

- **Statement.** For every k ≥ 0 the system x_i ⊕ x_(i+1 mod 2k+1) = 1 on 2k+1 variables, and the 9-variable Mermin-Peres parity system, are unsatisfiable while every drop-one subsystem is satisfiable. x⊕y⊕z, AND, OR and majority preserve one XOR, Horn, dual-Horn and 2-clause relation respectively. Every Boolean operation of any arity preserving both x∨y∨z and ¬x∨¬y∨¬z is a projection.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`PVsNP.odd_cycle_xor_maximally_contextual`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/SheafPolymorphism.lean#L167), [`PVsNP.mermin_peres_maximally_contextual`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/SheafPolymorphism.lean#L255), [`PVsNP.xorsat_maltsev`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/SheafPolymorphism.lean#L343), [`PVsNP.hornsat_and`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/SheafPolymorphism.lean#L364), [`PVsNP.dualhornsat_or`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/SheafPolymorphism.lean#L375), [`PVsNP.twosat_majority`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/SheafPolymorphism.lean#L386), [`PVsNP.monotone_3sat_only_projections`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/SheafPolymorphism.lean#L420), [`PVsNP.monotone_3sat_minimal`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/SheafPolymorphism.lean#L546); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound (C7); propext, Quot.sound (C6, C9); propext (C8).
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** 'Maximally contextual' in the Lean code means minimal unsatisfiability; no sheaf, empirical model or cohomology is formalized.
- **Limit.** This is not Schaefer's dichotomy theorem; no CSP complexity is formalized.
- **Limit.** k = 0 is the one-variable system x0 ⊕ x0 = 1.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="r30"></a>
### R30. Markov's negation bound for the inverter and a k-NOT compilation

- **Statement.** No circuit over constants, AND, OR and NOT with at most r NOT gates computes all n negations when n ≥ 2^r. An explicit circuit with exactly k NOT gates computes all negations of 2^k − 1 inputs. Every such circuit on 2^k − 1 inputs whose gates read only earlier wires compiles to a circuit with exactly k NOT gates that reproduces each of its wires; no size bound is proved.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Solution.prove_markov_inverter_tight`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/Solution.lean#L46), [`PVsNP.markov_inverter_lower`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/CircuitPhaseTransition.lean#L330), [`PVsNP.markov_inverter_upper`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/CircuitPhaseTransition.lean#L1119), [`PVsNP.fischer_negation_reduction`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/CircuitPhaseTransition.lean#L1403); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** Fischer's 1975 theorem is a polynomial-size construction; the Lean compilation has no size bound and its inverter is exponential-size.
- **Limit.** The upper bound and compilation cover n = 2^k − 1 only, not all n ≤ 2^k − 1.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`

<a id="r31"></a>
### R31. AND-count versus F2-degree, and permanent equals determinant modulo 2

- **Statement.** Every wire of a circuit over constants, XOR and AND with a AND gates computes an F2-polynomial of degree at most a + 1, so for n ≥ 1 computing the n-input AND needs at least n − 1 AND gates. For every n × n integer matrix, permanent ≡ determinant mod 2; for n ≥ 2 one 0/1 matrix has permanent 2 and determinant 0.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`PVsNP.mc_degree_lower_bound`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/CircuitPhaseTransition.lean#L2138), [`PVsNP.mc_andN_lower`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/CircuitPhaseTransition.lean#L2376), [`PVsNP.perm_eq_det_mod2`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/CircuitPhaseTransition.lean#L2413), [`PVsNP.perm_ne_det_witness`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/CircuitPhaseTransition.lean#L2510); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Permanent and determinant are defined by first-row Laplace expansion.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** No hardness notion for the permanent is formalized.
- **Limit.** The SAT-computed multiplicative-complexity values (experiment E3) are outside Lean and reproduce published classifications that are not cited.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="r32"></a>
### R32. Reversibility of straight-line register programs and one recursive product program

- **Statement.** Straight-line programs on integer registers built from add/subtract-constant and multiply-accumulate/subtract instructions, with destination distinct from operands, are undone by the reversed inverted program. For every nonempty list of d integers fixed in the program and every destination register with index ≥ d, an explicit program of 3·4^(d−1) − 2 instructions adds ± their product to that register and restores every other register, whatever their initial contents.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Solution.prove_catalytic_algebra`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/Solution.lean#L58), [`PVsNP.catalytic_program_reversible`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/CatalyticAlgebra.lean#L189), [`PVsNP.catalytic_multilinear_exact`](https://github.com/0thernes/p-vs-np-frontier-lab/blob/a70733fc388c24e3789eef9fb7afc8464f1669fe/lean/PVsNP/CatalyticAlgebra.lean#L279); axioms: propext, Quot.sound.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: overlapping earlier or parallel work found (see Limits).
- **Limit.** The multiplicands are program constants, so the value added is a constant; inputs are not read from registers.
- **Limit.** No space or time model and no catalytic-space complexity bound is formalized.
- **Limit.** Ben-Or and Cleve (1992) give register programs of polynomial length with three registers; that work is not cited.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="r33"></a>
### R33. Imaginary-gauge intertwining and zero-energy identities for the integer Hatano-Nelson chain

- **Statement.** For integers r, l and every N: the open Hatano-Nelson matrix with hoppings r², l² satisfies H·D = D·S entrywise for the gauge diagonal D and symmetric path S. Its characteristic recurrence satisfies P_2m(0) = (−1)^m (rl)^(2m), P_(2m+1)(0) = 0. The two-corner determinant formula holds for N = 3, 4. If 0 < l < r then l^(2m) < r^(2m).
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Monodromia.Palomar.t1_hatano_nelson_gbz`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L19), [`Monodromia.HatanoNelsonGBZ.hn_obc_matrix_similarity`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/HatanoNelsonGBZ.lean#L38), [`Monodromia.HatanoNelsonGBZ.hn_dual_corner_det4`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/HatanoNelsonGBZ.lean#L109), [`Monodromia.HatanoNelsonGBZ.hn_exponential_corner_catastrophe`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/HatanoNelsonGBZ.lean#L117); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Link from the corner formula to spectra of general N rests on a determinant identity proven only for N = 3, 4.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** The 'exponential corner catastrophe' conjunct is a power identity; no perturbed spectrum or eigenvalue is formalized.
- **Limit.** The dual-corner determinant formula is proven for N = 3 and 4 only.
- **Limit.** Integer parameters only; no floating-point or numerical-analysis statement is proven.
- **Limit.** The imaginary-gauge picture is classical (Hatano-Nelson); boundary fragility of the skin effect is in Guo et al., PRL 127, 116801 (2021).
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="r34"></a>
### R34. Eigenvector identities for a corner-perturbed Jordan block of any order

- **Statement.** For n ≥ 1 and integers λ₀, w: Nⁿ = 0 for the shift matrix with (N^(n−1))_(0,n−1) = 1; eigenvectors of J_n(λ₀) vanish beyond index 0; (w^j), (w^(n−1−j)) are right and left eigenvectors of J_n + wⁿe_(n−1)e₀ᵀ for λ₀ + w; ⟨L,R⟩² = n²w^(2n−2); |L|²|R|² = S_n(w²)²; for integer q > 0, n ≥ 2: S_n(q)² > 1, S_n(q) < S_(n+1)(q).
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Monodromia.Palomar.t2_exceptional_point_petermann`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L21), [`Monodromia.ExceptionalPoints.ep_puiseux_right_eigenvector`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/ExceptionalPoints.lean#L156), [`Monodromia.ExceptionalPoints.ep_petermann_exact_cancellation_bridge`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/ExceptionalPoints.lean#L354), [`Monodromia.ExceptionalPoints.ep_petermann_snr_strictly_below_one`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/ExceptionalPoints.lean#L343); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. The quotient SNR_Pet² = 1/S_n(q)² and any Petermann or sensitivity quantity are formed on paper, not in Lean.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: overlapping earlier or parallel work found (see Limits).
- **Limit.** Covers one model: a single Jordan block with the corner perturbation wⁿ, w an integer.
- **Limit.** No signal-to-noise ratio, Petermann factor or eigenvalue derivative is defined in Lean.
- **Limit.** Order-n Petermann-factor scaling is known at leading order (Wiersig, Phys. Rev. Research 5, 033042 (2023)); only the exact finite-q factor for this model may be new, and that was not searched.

<a id="r35"></a>
### R35. Lucas trace and determinant identities for powers of [[2,1],[1,1]]

- **Statement.** For M = [[2,1],[1,1]] and p ≥ 1: tr(Mᵖ) = L_2p and −det(I − Mᵖ) = L_2p − 2; L_2p − 2 ≠ L_2q − 2 for p ≠ q; L_4m − 2 is not the square of any integer for m ≥ 1. Two fixed reduced Burau matrices over Laurent polynomials satisfy the braid relation.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Monodromia.Palomar.t3b_burau_lucas_jones_and_fox_trapezoidal`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L25), [`Monodromia.SpectralKnots.burau_yang_baxter`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/SpectralKnots.lean#L73), [`Monodromia.SpectralKnots.gerono_knot_determinant_lucas`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/SpectralKnots.lean#L132), [`Monodromia.SpectralKnots.gerono_even_not_fox_milnor_square`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/SpectralKnots.lean#L313); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. M is not proven equal to the Burau image of σ₁σ₂⁻¹ at t = −1 in Lean; knot determinants and Fox-Milnor are cited, not formalized.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** Non-sliceness is not a Lean statement; via Fox-Milnor it applies only to even p not divisible by 3, since for 3 | p the closure of (σ₁σ₂⁻¹)ᵖ is a 3-component link.
- **Limit.** The values L_2p − 2 are known determinants of Turk's head links THK(3,p) (OEIS A004146, comment of 2014).
- **Limit.** The braid family is the known lemniscate-of-Gerono family (Bode, Dennis, Foster, King, arXiv:1611.02563, after Perron).
- **Limit.** This is not progress on the slice-ribbon conjecture (GEOM-038).
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="r36"></a>
### R36. Finite TL₃ bracket evaluations and trapezoidality of two coefficient lists

- **Statement.** In a hand-coded 5-dimensional Temperley-Lieb model TL₃: the closure brackets of (s₁s₂⁻¹)ᵖ for p = 1, 2, 3, 4 equal explicit Laurent lists; the first is 1, the others differ from it and are palindromic; the bracket of s₁³s₂ differs from that of its mirror. The list of 2m+1 ones and the list [p, 2p+1, p] (p ≥ 1) satisfy the trapezoidal predicate.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Monodromia.SpectralKnots.tl3_gerono_jones_polynomials`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/SpectralKnots.lean#L340), [`Monodromia.SpectralKnots.tl3_trefoil_chiral_separation`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/SpectralKnots.lean#L354), [`Monodromia.SpectralKnots.fox_trapezoidal_torus_2_odd`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/SpectralKnots.lean#L360), [`Monodromia.SpectralKnots.fox_trapezoidal_all_twist_knots`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/SpectralKnots.lean#L375); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Identification of the lists with Jones or Alexander polynomials of named knots is not formalized.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** The lists are typed in by hand; no knot, Alexander polynomial or Jones polynomial is defined in Lean.
- **Limit.** Torus knots T(2,2m+1) and twist knots are two-bridge, a class for which Fox's trapezoidal conjecture is already proved (atlas GEOM-046 row; Hartley 1979 per the literature report).
- **Limit.** [p, 2p+1, p] covers one parity of twist knots only (standard formula, not source-checked).
- **Limit.** 4₁, the Borromean rings and 8₁₈ are table knots/links; this has no bearing on the Jones unknot question (GEOM-039).
- **Limit.** The paper names the trefoil braid σ₁²σ₂ while Lean uses s₁³s₂.

<a id="r37"></a>
### R37. Discriminant sign and exceptional-point nilpotency for a non-reciprocal 2×2 integer system

- **Statement.** For integers r_A, r_B, J_AB, J_BA: J_AB·J_BA ≥ 0 implies (r_A − r_B)² + 4J_AB·J_BA ≥ 0; if that discriminant is 0, S = [[r_A−r_B, 2J_AB],[2J_BA, r_B−r_A]] satisfies S² = 0, and S ≠ 0 unless J_AB = J_BA = 0. With r_A = r_B = R², J_AB = J, J_BA = −J, ψ_A = isR, ψ_B = R and s² = 1, the cubic field is tangent at both sites, each with moment −sJR².
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Monodromia.Palomar.t4_non_reciprocal_cep_and_chiral_matter`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L27), [`Monodromia.NonReciprocalCEP.nr_cep_jordan_defective`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/NonReciprocalCEP.lean#L29), [`Monodromia.NonReciprocalCEP.nr_chiral_quadrature_time_crystal`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/NonReciprocalCEP.lean#L52); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Pointwise algebraic identities only; no differential equation, orbit or stability statement.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** No time crystal, limit cycle or attraction is formalized.
- **Limit.** The 'non-variational curl' conjunct is J_AB ≠ J_BA ⇒ J_AB − J_BA ≠ 0, with the curl defined as subtraction.
- **Limit.** The discriminant facts are textbook properties of real 2×2 matrices.
- **Limit.** Atlas CM-043 asks about many-body time crystals and excludes limit-cycle synchronization; this result does not bear on it.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

### R38. Intertwining metric and its positivity for the PT-symmetric dimer over the Gaussian integers

- **Statement.** For integers g, k with H = [[ig, k],[k, −ig]] and η = [[k, −ig],[ig, k]] over ℤ[i]: ηH = H†η; if 0 < k and g² < k², then Re(v†ηv) > 0 for every nonzero Gaussian-integer vector v; at g = k ≠ 0 the vector (1, −i) has η-norm 0.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Monodromia.Palomar.t5_pt_symmetry_and_pseudo_hermitian_metric`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L29), [`Monodromia.PTSymmetryMetric.pt_metric_intertwining`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/PTSymmetryMetric.lean#L22), [`Monodromia.PTSymmetryMetric.pt_unbroken_metric_pos_def`](https://github.com/0thernes/monodromia/blob/153792bdaee2b01f9a56afbcb60af7d2957d5669/lean/MonodromiaProofs/PTSymmetryMetric.lean#L43); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Reality of eigenvalues and impossibility of a positive metric in the broken phase are paper-level; their Lean conjuncts are scalar arithmetic.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** Positivity is shown over Gaussian-integer vectors and integer parameters, not complex vectors or real parameters.
- **Limit.** The T5 conjuncts named 'universal pseudo-Hermitian reality' and 'broken-PT metric impossibility' do not quantify over metrics; the latter holds with the PT hypotheses removed.
- **Limit.** The dimer and its metric are the standard example (Bender-Boettcher 1998; Mostafazadeh 2002).

<a id="r39"></a>
### R39. Kernel-checked lower bounds for C₂₇ and C₃₁ that are weaker than published values

- **Statement.** C₂₇^⊠2048 and C₃₁^⊠2048 contain independent sets of sizes M₂₇ and M₃₁ with 13319390984674515^2048 ≤ M₂₇·10^(15·2048) and 15308776193986504^2048 ≤ M₃₁·10^(15·2048). Consequently Θ(C₂₇) ≥ 13.319390984674515 and Θ(C₃₁) ≥ 15.308776193986504. Both are smaller than 31522^(1/4) ≈ 13.3246 and 54934^(1/4) ≈ 15.3095, the values in Codenotti–Gerace–Resta 2003, Table 1.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`ZeroErrorFrontier.WorldRecords.t2b_c27_world_record`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/WorldRecords.lean#L1833), [`ZeroErrorFrontier.WorldRecords.t2d_c31_world_record`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/WorldRecords.lean#L1875), [`ZeroErrorFrontier.CosetQuotient.t1f_baumert_pullback_homomorphism`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/CosetQuotient.lean#L1693); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Independent construction (Baumert expansion plus port systems); it does not formalise the 2003 sets and gives no new bound.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** These are not best known bounds: the 2003 table lists α(C₂₇^⊠4) ≥ 31522 and α(C₃₁^⊠4) ≥ 54934, above the repository's fourth-power bases 31466 and 54913 and above its final bounds.
- **Limit.** The 2003 entries are printed cardinalities marked as obtained by expansion formulas and experiment; the explicit sets were not examined in this audit.
- **Limit.** Θ is not defined in Lean; the expansion map is the known construction of Baumert et al. (1971).
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`

<a id="r40"></a>
### R40. Coset codes in the fourth strong power of C_n for n = 8k+1 with published cardinalities

- **Statement.** For every k ≥ 3 and n = 8k+1: α(C_n^⊠4) ≥ (4k² + ⌊k/2⌋)·n². The set is lifted from 4k² + ⌊k/2⌋ = ⌊n⌊n/8⌋/2⌋ points of ℤ_n², no two within circular distance 1 in coordinate one and 7 in coordinate two; no such point set is larger (n ≥ 8), and each one of size M lifts to M·n² points (n ≥ 15).
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`ZeroErrorFrontier.CosetQuotient.t1e_infinite_hstar_family_and_ports`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/CosetQuotient.lean#L1206), [`ZeroErrorFrontier.CosetQuotient.t1a_coset_quotient_compression`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/CosetQuotient.lean#L249), [`ZeroErrorFrontier.CosetQuotient.t1b_exact_quotient_ceilings`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/CosetQuotient.lean#L533); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Same cardinalities as published bounds, obtained from cosets of ⟨(1,0,0,2),(0,1,0,4)⟩.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** For odd k the size equals n²(n²−n−8)/16, Theorem 1 (d = 3) of Codenotti, Gerace and Resta (2003); for even k it equals (n⁴−n³)/16, the Baumert et al. (1971) value.
- **Limit.** The repository does not cite the 2003 paper.
- **Limit.** The contract statement contains conflict-pattern facts for odd k but no port system; the n²-port system is a separate kernel-checked definition outside the audited 32 declarations.
- **Limit.** The companion theorem T1c is narrower than its prose: one of two claimed branches, n ≥ 9, ball sizes at four n.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="r41"></a>
### R41. Haemers rank bound, Haemers's Schläfli-graph example and Lovász's pentagon bound

- **Statement.** If a matrix LR over a field, of inner width r, has nonzero diagonal and vanishes on all pairs of distinct non-adjacent vertices of G, then α(G^⊠d) ≤ r^d for all d. For the Schläfli graph S and its complement S̄: α(S^⊠d) ≤ 3^d, α(S̄^⊠d) ≤ 7^d and α(S ⊠ S̄) ≥ 27 > 21. For the pentagon: α(C₅^⊠2) ≥ 5 and α(C₅^⊠d)² ≤ 5^d.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`ZeroErrorFrontier.HaemersParadox.t4a_haemers_rank_theorem`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/HaemersParadox.lean#L320), [`ZeroErrorFrontier.HaemersParadox.t4d_haemers_schlafli_paradox`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/HaemersParadox.lean#L1849), [`ZeroErrorFrontier.HaemersParadox.t4c_lovasz_pentagon_theorem`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/HaemersParadox.lean#L1404), [`ZeroErrorFrontier.HaemersParadox.t4b_integer_orthogonal_handle_sandwich`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/HaemersParadox.lean#L558); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Results of Haemers (1979) and Lovász (1979); integer forms, without real numbers.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** Θ and √5 are not defined in Lean; Θ(C₅) = √5 and Θ(S ⊠ S̄) > Θ(S)·Θ(S̄) are consequences outside Lean.
- **Limit.** That the defined 27-vertex graph is strongly regular (27,16,10,8) was checked by computation outside Lean; uniqueness of that graph is a cited fact.
- **Limit.** The orthogonal-representation bound T4b is proved for vectors of equal norm, while the paper states it for bounded norm.
- **Limit.** The claim to be the first such formal proof was not verified; the 1979 articles were not opened in this audit.

<a id="r42"></a>
### R42. Two-factor combining rule for separation systems, table admissibility, and the seven-label graph

- **Statement.** If label systems are realised by independent families in G₁, G₂, and a table gives each target label a list of label pairs, where any two pairs in one list, or in the lists of two separated target labels, are separated in some coordinate, then product blocks realise the target system in G₁ ⊠ G₂ with sizes Σ|R₁(a)|·|R₂(b)|. Five fixed tables pass the checks. For the seven-label graph G₇: α(G₇) ≥ 3, α(G₇^⊠d) ≤ 3^d.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`ZeroErrorFrontier.SeparationCalculus.t3c_heterogeneous_composition_and_tables`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/SeparationCalculus.lean#L1071), [`ZeroErrorFrontier.SeparationCalculus.t3a_g7_induced_c5_and_exact_capacity`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/SeparationCalculus.lean#L260), [`ZeroErrorFrontier.SeparationCalculus.t3b_weighted_fractional_clique_barrier`](https://github.com/0thernes/zero-error-torus-frontier/blob/af343aaa1701637e23733ac0abd7a8f9b2e22b60/lean/ZeroErrorFrontier/SeparationCalculus.lean#L404); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Two-input case of the BPZ combining rule (Tandon 2026, Proposition 1), allowing two alphabets.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** This does not resolve the two-sided extension of Theorem 1 that Tandon (arXiv:2608.30273, Section 5 and Remark 3) leaves for future work: no Gao gadget, compatibility condition or profile formula is derived.
- **Limit.** The same rule exists for one alphabet in Tandon's Proposition 1 and in the Buys–Polak–Zuiddam Lean library (Realisation.multiSubst); the general theorem is never instantiated in the repository.
- **Limit.** The tables are those of Buys, Polak and Zuiddam; only their admissibility is re-checked.
- **Limit.** The G₇ bound is a 3-clique cover of the repository's own auxiliary graph; the weighted clique-cover theorem T3b gives upper bounds only (46875/2 is not shown optimal).
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="r48"></a>
### R48. Spectral floor for arbitrary Pauli sets, its Kramers variant, and saturation conditions

- **Statement.** For N ≥ 1, every set S of non-identity N-qubit Pauli labels and every ±1 assignment f on S satisfy (2^N−1)β₆ ≤ (2^N−2)|S|√((2^N−1)|S|), where β₆ = 6·(satisfied − violated commuting 3-point lines in S). For S inside an elliptic quadric (N ≥ 2) the same holds with 2^(N−1). For nonempty S, equality is equivalent to four spectral or flat-state conditions, and for N ≥ 2 it forces (2^N−1)|S| to be a square.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`HoggarBound.hoggar_bound`](https://github.com/0thernes/pauli-contextuality-lab/blob/b0f8a180f441674c439840d5125e2c4dcac55a18/lean/HoggarBound/Bound.lean#L169), [`HoggarBound.hoggar_bound_quaternionic`](https://github.com/0thernes/pauli-contextuality-lab/blob/b0f8a180f441674c439840d5125e2c4dcac55a18/lean/HoggarBound/Kramers.lean#L439), [`HoggarBound.saturation_tfae`](https://github.com/0thernes/pauli-contextuality-lab/blob/b0f8a180f441674c439840d5125e2c4dcac55a18/lean/HoggarBound/SaturationTheory.lean#L471), [`HoggarBound.saturation_mul_isSquare`](https://github.com/0thernes/pauli-contextuality-lab/blob/b0f8a180f441674c439840d5125e2c4dcac55a18/lean/HoggarBound/SaturationTheory.lean#L822), [`PauliContextualityLab.ComparatorTarget.target_spectral_floor`](https://github.com/0thernes/pauli-contextuality-lab/blob/b0f8a180f441674c439840d5125e2c4dcac55a18/lean/Solution.lean#L29); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. The floor and its Kramers variant are Jonah's prior results (doi:10.5281/zenodo.23538829); the stated contribution is the formalization and arbitrary supports.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Re-audit pending.** Audited at commit `b0f8a18`; the archive pin has since moved to `9d57651`.
- **Limit.** The repository's paper calls the bounds prior work of Jonah; README, CLAIMS.md and the hub list them as results without that label.
- **Limit.** Jonah's preprint abstract states an attainment criterion (violation-regular assignment with simple top eigenvalue) for closed families; its overlap with the five-way equivalence was not compared line by line.
- **Limit.** The perfect-square condition is an elementary consequence of β₆ being an integer.
- **Limit.** Contexts are only commuting 3-point lines {u, v, u+v}; an independent Lean formalization of a similar floor was deposited on Zenodo after the pinned commit (doi:10.5281/zenodo.24567331).

### R49. Kernel-checked lower bounds for the contextuality degrees of W(7,2) and Q⁺(9,2)

- **Statement.** Every ±1 assignment on the 255 non-identity four-qubit Pauli labels violates at least 1455 commuting 3-point lines of W(7,2), and every ±1 assignment on the non-identity five-qubit labels with q₀ = 0 violates at least 6444 lines of Q⁺(9,2).
- **Related source.** [Z. Li, A. Gupta, V. Ganesh, The Four-Qubit Hyperbolic Quadric Has Contextuality Degree 315 (2026), arXiv:2610.26465](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26465v1), Section Open problems, items 1 and 2.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`HoggarBound.ExactDegreesN4N5.w72_ge_1455`](https://github.com/0thernes/pauli-contextuality-lab/blob/b0f8a180f441674c439840d5125e2c4dcac55a18/lean/HoggarBound/ExactDegreesN4N5.lean#L1078), [`HoggarBound.ExactDegreesN4N5.q9plus_ge_6444`](https://github.com/0thernes/pauli-contextuality-lab/blob/b0f8a180f441674c439840d5125e2c4dcac55a18/lean/HoggarBound/ExactDegreesN4N5.lean#L1088), [`PauliContextualityLab.ComparatorTarget.target_w72_ge_1455`](https://github.com/0thernes/pauli-contextuality-lab/blob/b0f8a180f441674c439840d5125e2c4dcac55a18/lean/Solution.lean#L94), [`PauliContextualityLab.ComparatorTarget.target_q9plus_ge_6444`](https://github.com/0thernes/pauli-contextuality-lab/blob/b0f8a180f441674c439840d5125e2c4dcac55a18/lean/Solution.lean#L99); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. The repository's exact values 1575 and 6975 are proved in Lean only conditionally on d(Q⁻(7,2)) ≥ 315, which rests on a C census.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: overlapping earlier or parallel work found (see Limits).
- **Re-audit pending.** Audited at commit `b0f8a18`; the archive pin has since moved to `9d57651`.
- **Limit.** These are lower bounds; Li, Gupta and Ganesh ask for the exact degrees. 1455 raises their stated lower bound 1071 for W(7,2), and 6444 > 4743 gives a negative answer to their question whether deg Q⁺(9,2) = 4743.
- **Limit.** Jonah's unreviewed, computer-assisted note of 20 October 2026 (doi:10.5281/zenodo.23510634) already claimed the exact values 1575 and 6975 and the negative answer; README and CLAIMS.md do not say so.
- **Limit.** The repository's own notes state these bounds should not be presented as new resolutions.

### R60. Square-lattice obstruction for an isotropic quadratic-plus-periodic integer function

- **Statement.** There is no integer function u on ℤ² with 4u = 3(x²+y²) + ψ, ψ doubly periodic with periods ≥ 1, whose square-lattice Laplacian is at most 3 everywhere. Four explicit integer functions have constant Laplacian 2 or 3.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declaration [`ApollonianSandpileMarkov.Palomar.t2c_lps_z2_superharmonic_and_isotropic_obstruction`](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov/blob/94ece6a8dce9c9a30c6a2ada2eb335cb0501f770/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L41); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: overlapping earlier or parallel work found (see Limits).
- **Re-audit pending.** Audited at commit `94ece6a`; the archive pin has since moved to `bc6464b`.
- **Limit.** One isotropic quadratic of the exact form (3(x²+y²)+ψ)/4, not the general problem: the set of quadratic growths of integer superharmonic functions on ℤ² was determined by Levine, Pegden and Smart (Ann. of Math. 186 (2017), arXiv:1309.3267).
- **Limit.** Filed with known mathematics because the set of attainable quadratic growths on ℤ² is classified by Levine, Pegden and Smart (2017); whether this exact case follows from that classification was not checked, and no prior-work search is recorded.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`

<a id="r62"></a>
### R62. Hantzsche–Wendt group model and its 4×4 Bloch evaluation map

- **Statement.** A concrete multiplication on ℤ³ × {e,a,b,ab} satisfies the group axioms and the relations a⁻¹b²a = b⁻², b⁻¹a²b = a⁻², and is non-abelian and torsion-free. Over any commutative ring in the repository's class, 4×4 Bloch evaluation at points with given inverses preserves 1, + and ×; a separately defined formal coefficient map is injective.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`PromislowFracton.Palomar.t1a_promislow_group_torsion_free`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L19), [`PromislowFracton.Palomar.t1b_faithful_four_band_bloch_homomorphism`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L21), [`PromislowFracton.ElemP.torsion_free`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PromislowFracton/PromislowGroup.lean#L219), [`PromislowFracton.GroupRingElem.psiFormal_injective`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PromislowFracton/BlochMatrix.lean#L452); axioms: t1a: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound; t1b: propext, Quot.sound. Faithfulness is proved for a separate coefficient map, not for the evaluation map.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** The isomorphism between the normal-form model and the presented group is not formalized.
- **Limit.** No lemma links the injective coefficient map psiFormal to the evaluation map, and over ℤ the evaluation map alone is not injective.
- **Limit.** The 4×4 representation is standard (Passman, arXiv:2108.06570) and is not cited by the repository.

<a id="r63"></a>
### R63. Gardam's unit in F₂[P]: inverse check, reversible cellular automaton, lift determinant data

- **Statement.** Gardam's 21-term α, β satisfy αβ = βα = 1 in F₂[P]. The repository's sparse 4×4 Laurent determinant of the 0/1 lift of α has 89 terms, constant term 97 and coefficient sum 945; it is V₄-invariant and ≡ 1 mod 2 and mod 4. Convolutions by α and β on configurations P → F₂ are mutually inverse, and convolution by α commutes with left shifts.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`PromislowFracton.Palomar.t1e_gardam_unit_mod4_and_hensel_tower`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L27), [`PromislowFracton.Palomar.t1g_surjunctive_reversible_linear_ca`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L31), [`PromislowFracton.gardam_det_mod4_and_v4_law`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PromislowFracton/GardamUnit.lean#L206), [`PromislowFracton.gardam_linear_ca_bijective`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PromislowFracton/GardamUnit.lean#L866); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. The 2-adic Hensel tower and inverse support sizes are Rust/Python computations; Lean has only a generic identity.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** The unit αβ = 1 in F₂[P] is Gardam's (arXiv:2102.11818).
- **Limit.** The theorem named for the Hensel tower contains no Hensel statement; the generic identity is never applied to Gardam's unit.
- **Limit.** No theorem links the determinant routine to the Bloch evaluation map.
- **Limit.** Surjunctivity is already known for the sofic group P; the cellular automaton is the standard one attached to a unit.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="r64"></a>
### R64. Monomials are non-constant on a rational parametrization of Haah's CC1 curve

- **Statement.** Let K be an integral domain with 2 ≠ 0 in which Px = −(1+3t+t²), Py = 1+t−t² and Pz = −1+t+t² each have a root, and let D = 1+t+t². If Px^a·Py^b·Pz^c·D^(−a−b−c), cleared of negative powers, equals a nonzero constant for every t ∈ K, then a = b = c = 0. The analogous statement holds on one explicit line over F₄.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`PromislowFracton.Palomar.t2c_universal_all_prime_no_strings`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L37), [`PromislowFracton.universal_all_prime_no_strings_theorem`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PromislowFracton/HaahFractons.lean#L487); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. The reduction from string logical operators to this monomial condition is on paper only.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: overlapping earlier or parallel work found (see Limits).
- **Limit.** String logical operators are not defined in Lean.
- **Limit.** The hypotheses fail over ℤ, ℚ and F_p with p ≡ ±2 mod 5 (the quadratics have discriminant 5), so those cases need an extension field.
- **Limit.** Characteristic 2 is covered only by one line over F₄.
- **Limit.** The qubit no-strings property is Haah's (arXiv:1101.1962), and Kim (arXiv:1202.0052) gives no-string conditions for qudit cubic codes; the repository compares with neither.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="r65"></a>
### R65. Unit-twisted CSS commutation identity and two explicit CSS pairs over ℤ[P]

- **Statement.** In any associative structure satisfying the listed involution axioms, fg = gf and uv = 1 imply (fu)(g*v*)* − (gu)(f*v*)* = 0. For Haah's f, g on P twisted by Gardam's α, β, the four check polynomials have 52, 52, 52 and 56 terms mod 2; the commutator vanishes mod 2 and has 342 terms over ℤ. A second explicit pair commutes in ℤ[P] and is not central.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`PromislowFracton.Palomar.t2a_nonabelian_unit_twisted_css_commutation`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L33), [`PromislowFracton.Palomar.t2e_nonabelian_hw_fracton_codes`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L41), [`PromislowFracton.gardam_twisted_and_intrinsic_hw_fracton_codes`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PromislowFracton/HaahFractons.lean#L832); axioms: t2a: none; t2e: propext. No code distance, logical-qubit count or fracton property is formalized.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: overlapping earlier or parallel work found (see Limits).
- **Limit.** The identity also holds for u = 1, so commutation does not depend on Gardam's unit.
- **Limit.** The paper's k(M_L) = 4·k_Haah(L) is not in Lean.
- **Limit.** Related prior constructions exist (Tian–Samperton–Wang arXiv:1812.02101; Lin–Pryadko arXiv:2306.16400); 'first' is unsupported.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`
- **Source.** [promislow-fracton-amoeba](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba) `v1.0.0` · archive entry `0TH-2026-015` · [paper](papers/0TH-2026-015_promislow-fracton-amoeba/Donahue_2026_promislow_fracton_amoeba.pdf)

<a id="r66"></a>
### R66. Origin minimum of symmetric midpoint-convex functions and generation of S₄ by two permutations

- **Statement.** Every R : ℚ³ → ℚ that respects rational equality, is midpoint convex and is invariant under the three double sign flips satisfies R(0,0,0) ≤ R(x,y,z). In a 4-tuple model of S₄, the adjacent transpositions satisfy the braid relations; (1 2) and (0 2 1 3) do not commute, satisfy g₁² = id and g₂⁴ = id, and generate all 24 permutations.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`PromislowFracton.Palomar.t3a_v4_convex_ronkin_origin_minimum`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L43), [`PromislowFracton.Palomar.t3c_artin_b4_and_full_s4_monodromy`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L47), [`PromislowFracton.v4_convex_ronkin_origin_minimum`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PromislowFracton/NonHermitianAmoeba.lean#L73), [`PromislowFracton.artin_b4_relations_and_s4_monodromy_generation`](https://github.com/0thernes/promislow-fracton-amoeba/blob/c829ab811568a13e2283db2175f684e35b17c816/lean/PromislowFracton/NonHermitianAmoeba.lean#L274); axioms: t3a: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound; t3c: propext. Ronkin functions and the monodromy of Ψ(α̃) appear only in Rust/Python numerics.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** The minimum is not strict: the constant function satisfies all hypotheses.
- **Limit.** No Ronkin function or real-valued function is formalized.
- **Limit.** No braid group B₄ is defined, and that the two permutations are monodromies is numerical.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="r67"></a>
### R67. Integer octonion composition law, Moufang identities, non-associativity and a sedenion zero divisor

- **Statement.** For the 8-dimensional algebra on Z^8 given by the stated Fano table: N(xy) = N(x)N(y) and the left, right and middle Moufang identities hold for all x, y, z; the associator of e1, e2, e3 is nonzero. In the integer Cayley-Dickson sedenions, (e1+e10)(e3+e14) = 0, and so are all nonzero integer rescalings of this pair.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Octonionica.OctonionsSedenions.oct_degen_graves_eight_square`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/OctonionsSedenions.lean#L31), [`Octonionica.OctonionsSedenions.oct_left_moufang`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/OctonionsSedenions.lean#L313), [`Octonionica.OctonionsSedenions.oct_right_moufang`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/OctonionsSedenions.lean#L334), [`Octonionica.OctonionsSedenions.oct_middle_moufang`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/OctonionsSedenions.lean#L299), [`Octonionica.OctonionsSedenions.oct_strictly_non_associative`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/OctonionsSedenions.lean#L52), [`Octonionica.OctonionsSedenions.sedenion_zero_divisor_collapse`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/OctonionsSedenions.lean#L346), [`Octonionica.OctonionsSedenions.sedenion_infinite_zero_divisor_family`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/OctonionsSedenions.lean#L356); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.
- **Audit (2026-11-27).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** Proved over Z (integer coordinates) only; the extension to real octonions by the polynomial-identity argument is not formalized.
- **Limit.** Classical results (eight-square identity, Moufang 1935, Cayley-Dickson); an Isabelle AFP formalization of octonions (2018) already exists.
- **Limit.** The 'infinite 2-parameter family' of sedenion zero divisors consists only of integer rescalings c*u, d*v of one pair.

### R68. 7D cross product, unimodular rank-14 g2 = Der(O(Z)), and cleared-denominator S^6 Nijenhuis norm identity

- **Statement.** Over Z^7: |u x v|^2 = |u|^2|v|^2 - <u,v>^2, <u x v, w> is totally skew, 2J(u,v,w) = -3 Im[u,v,w]. Octonion derivations Der(O(Z)) form a rank-14 Z-module with unique unimodular basis g2Basis, closed under Lie bracket, preserving the 3-form phi infinitesimally. For X, Y orthogonal to p, N(p;X,Y) satisfies |N|^2 = 16|p|^6(|X|^2|Y|^2 - <X,Y>^2) - 16|p|^4<p x X, Y>^2, strictly positive on complex-orthogonal pairs.

- **Limit.** Non-integrability of the octonionic almost complex structure on S^6 is classical (Eckmann-Frolicher and Ehresmann-Libermann 1951); the norm identity is Gray's constant-type condition. The existence of other complex structures on S^6 stays open and is not addressed.

<a id="r69"></a>
### R69. 27D Albert Jordan algebra J3(O(Z)): universal Jordan identity, Freudenthal cubic, and Peirce primitivity

- **Statement.** For all Hermitian 3x3 matrices X, Y over O(Z) with X∘₂Y = XY+YX: X∘₂Y = Y∘₂X, X∘₂X# = 2det(X)I, X³ − 2Tr(X)X² + 4S(X)X − 4det(X)I = 0, and universally ((X∘₂X)∘₂Y)∘₂X = (X∘₂X)∘₂(Y∘₂X). For all u, v ∈ O(Z), P(u,v) has P# = 0, det P = 0, P∘₂P = 2Tr(P)P, and universal Peirce primitivity 2(P∘₂(P∘₂X)) − (P∘₂P)∘₂X = 2Tr(P∘₂X)P.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Octonionica.AlbertJordan.albert_mul2_comm`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/AlbertJordan.lean#L48), [`Octonionica.AlbertJordan.albert_freudenthal_adjoint_identity`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/AlbertJordan.lean#L213), [`Octonionica.AlbertJordan.albert_cubic_cayley_hamilton`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/AlbertJordan.lean#L261), [`Octonionica.AlbertJordan.albert_op2_primitive_idempotent`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/AlbertJordan.lean#L308), [`Octonionica.AlbertJordan.albert_adjoint_jordan_assoc`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/AlbertJordan.lean#L991), [`Octonionica.AlbertJordan.albert_universal_jordan_identity`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/AlbertJordan.lean#L1049), [`Octonionica.AlbertJordan.albert_op2_peirce_primitivity`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/AlbertJordan.lean#L1397); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.
- **Audit (2026-11-27).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** Over Z (cleared denominators) only; over Q, e = P(u,v)/Tr(P) is a primitive idempotent (e^2 = e, U_e(X) = Tr(e o X)e), while the smooth projective manifold OP^2 is not formalized.
- **Limit.** Classical results of Jordan-von Neumann-Wigner (1934), Albert (1934), and Freudenthal (1985).

### R70. 2E8 Z-basis and 240-shell, Golay [24,12,8] parity/encoding equivalence, and 24D Conway Leech lattice

- **Statement.** In Z^8, 2E8 is even (|x|^2 = 0 mod 8), uniquely Z-spanned by eight roots with Gram matrix 4*G_E8 (det = 1), and its exact 240-vector minimal shell is duplicate-free. In F2^24, Golay parity check equals golayEncode image with weights (1, 759, 2576, 759, 1). Conway's 24D Leech lattice sqrt(8)*Lambda_24 is closed under +/-, even (|x|^2 = 0 mod 16), rootless (|x|^2 >= 32), with 3-shape minimal-shell classification.

<a id="r71"></a>
### R71. Ramanujan tau: Euler product equality, mod-691 congruence, and non-vanishing for n at most 20

- **Statement.** Truncated at q^20, (E4^3 - E6^2)/1728 is integral and equals Ramanujan's Euler product q*prod_{m=1}^{20}(1-q^m)^24 identically (propext only); tau(1..12) = 1, -24, 252, -1472, 4830, -6048, -16744, 84480, -113643, -115920, 534612, -370944; for 1 <= n <= 20, tau(n) != 0 and tau(n) = sigma_11(n) mod 691; four Hecke instances (n = 4, 6, 9, 10) hold.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Octonionica.E8GolayMoonshine.ramanujan_tau_exact_mod691_and_lehmer`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/E8GolayMoonshine.lean#L567), [`Octonionica.E8GolayMoonshine.ramanujan_tau_eq_euler_product_20`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/E8GolayMoonshine.lean#L584); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Lean range is n <= 20; the Rust check to n = 120 and Deligne primes <= 113 lie outside Lean.
- **Audit (2026-11-27).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** Lehmer's conjecture (tau(n) != 0 for all n) remains open; the checked range (n <= 20 in Lean, n <= 120 in Rust) is far below published verification ranges and is a sanity check, not progress.
- **Limit.** In v1.2.0, exact equality of (E4^3 - E6^2)/1728 with q*prod(1-q^m)^24 is proved through q^20 (ramanujan_tau_eq_euler_product_20, propext only); general Hecke multiplicativity and modular transformation laws remain unformalized.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build --wfail && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`

<a id="r72"></a>
### R72. Fibonacci anyon F and R matrices: coherence identities and infinite order of a braid word

- **Statement.** For explicit 2x2 matrices F, R over Z[zeta_10] (reduced modulo Phi_10): F^2 = I, the direct 3x3 matrix Mac Lane pentagon equation M2(F)*M1(F) = R3(F)*M1(F)*M2(F) built from the entries of F holds with zero axioms, both hexagon identities hold in the stated gauge, the Artin B3 braid relation s1 s2 s1 = s2 s1 s2 holds with s1 = R, s2 = F R F, and for every k >= 1, (s1 s2^-1)^k != I.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Octonionica.FibonacciAnyons.fib_mac_lane_pentagon_matrix`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/FibonacciAnyons.lean#L105), [`Octonionica.FibonacciAnyons.fib_coherence_hexagon_and_yang_baxter`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/FibonacciAnyons.lean#L86), [`Octonionica.FibonacciAnyons.fib_braid_word_infinite_order`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/FibonacciAnyons.lean#L258); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.
- **Audit (2026-11-27).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** In v1.1.0, the 3x3 matrix Mac Lane pentagon equation built directly from fibF (fib_mac_lane_pentagon_matrix, 0 axioms) is included in comparator target T5 alongside the retained scalar component identities.
- **Limit.** Infinite order follows from a standard trace argument (Tr W = 2 - phi, whose Galois conjugate 1 + phi exceeds 2), which is how Lean proves it. Density of the Fibonacci braid representation (Freedman-Larsen-Wang 2002) is classical and not formalized.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build --wfail && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`
- **Source.** [octonionica](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica) `v1.2.0` · archive entry `0TH-2026-017` · [paper](papers/0TH-2026-017_octonionica/Donahue_2026_Octonionica.pdf)

<a id="r73"></a>
### R73. Markov equation: Vieta descent, congruences mod 3 and mod 4, Fibonacci and Pell branches, shared-maximum divisibility

- **Statement.** For integers with x^2 + y^2 + z^2 = 3xyz: if 0 < x <= y < z then 0 < 3xy - z < z; positive solutions have no entry divisible by 3, and their residues mod 4 are (1,1,1) or a permutation of (2,1,1); (1, F(2k+1), F(2k+3)) and (2, P(2k+1), P(2k+3)) are solutions for all k; two solutions sharing z satisfy z | (x1y2 - x2y1)(x1y2 + x2y1).
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Octonionica.MarkovTree.markov_strict_descent`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/MarkovTree.lean#L31), [`Octonionica.MarkovTree.markov_no_multiple_of_three`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/MarkovTree.lean#L77), [`Octonionica.MarkovTree.markov_positive_mod4_classification`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/MarkovTree.lean#L207), [`Octonionica.MarkovTree.markov_fibonacci_branch`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/MarkovTree.lean#L344), [`Octonionica.MarkovTree.markov_pell_branch`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/MarkovTree.lean#L365), [`Octonionica.MarkovTree.markov_uniqueness_divisor_obstruction`](https://github.com/0thernes/octonionica/blob/e064cb44d0f4dff8bf9021f7a822d3517efe8e68/lean/OctonionicaProofs/MarkovTree.lean#L388); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.
- **Audit (2026-11-27).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** The Markov uniqueness conjecture remains open; the divisor relation is a necessary condition that follows from x^2 + y^2 = 0 mod z and holds for identical or permuted triples, so it does not constrain counterexamples by itself.
- **Limit.** The 'Hurwitz sqrt(5) barrier' theorem is the integer inequality 0 < 9m^2 - 4 < 9m^2 and is not covered by this record.
- **Limit.** The Rust BFS over the 231 triples with maximum <= 10^15 is outside Lean and far below published verification ranges.
- **Limit.** Congruences go back to Frobenius (1913), cited in the v1.1.0/v1.2.0 manuscript.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build --wfail && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`
