<a id="r74"></a>
### R74. Jacobi symbol reciprocity laws and uniqueness of square roots of -1 modulo odd prime powers

- **Statement.** For odd n ≥ 1 the Jacobi symbol (built from Euler's criterion) satisfies the (−1/n) and (2/n) laws, multiplicativity, (a²/n) = 1 when gcd(a,n) = 1, and reciprocity for coprime odd m, n ≥ 1. For an odd prime p and k ≥ 1, if pᵏ divides r₁²+1 and r₂²+1 then pᵏ divides r₁−r₂ or r₁+r₂.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`ApollonianSandpileMarkov.Palomar.t1b_jacobi_reciprocity_master`](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov/blob/94ece6a8dce9c9a30c6a2ada2eb335cb0501f770/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L23), [`ApollonianSandpileMarkov.Palomar.t3c_hensel_sqrt_neg1_prime_power_uniqueness`](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov/blob/94ece6a8dce9c9a30c6a2ada2eb335cb0501f770/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L59); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Zero-Mathlib Lean 4.35.0 development.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Re-audit pending.** Audited at commit `94ece6a`; the archive pin has since moved to `bc6464b`.
- **Limit.** Classical results (Gauss, Jacobi, Eisenstein 1844 proof method); the contribution is a Mathlib-free formalization.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`
- **Source.** [apollonian-sandpile-markov](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov) `v3.0.0` · archive entry `0TH-2026-018` · [paper](papers/0TH-2026-018_apollonian-sandpile-markov/Donahue_2026_apollonian_sandpile_markov.pdf)

<a id="r75"></a>
### R75. Absence of square curvatures in three integral Apollonian circle packings

- **Statement.** In the Apollonian packings generated by (−3,5,8,8), (−7,17,12,20) and (−11,21,24,28) — every quadruple reachable from the root by the four Descartes reflections — no curvature equals m² for any integer m. Supporting identities: the reflections are involutions preserving the Descartes form, and (a+b)(a+c) = a² + k² for Descartes quadruples.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`ApollonianSandpileMarkov.Palomar.t1c_hkrs_apollonian_reciprocity_obstruction`](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov/blob/94ece6a8dce9c9a30c6a2ada2eb335cb0501f770/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L26), [`ApollonianSandpileMarkov.Palomar.t1a_descartes_lorentzian_and_gaussian_norm`](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov/blob/94ece6a8dce9c9a30c6a2ada2eb335cb0501f770/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L20); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Formalizes Haag-Kertzer-Rickards-Stange Theorem 1.6 and two Table A instances.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Re-audit pending.** Audited at commit `94ece6a`; the archive pin has since moved to `bc6464b`.
- **Limit.** Covers three specific packings, not the HKRS families of obstructed packings.
- **Limit.** The result is due to Haag, Kertzer, Rickards and Stange (Ann. of Math. 2024, arXiv:2307.02749); only the zero-Mathlib proof route is new.
- **Limit.** The contrast packing rootSquareContrast = (−4,8,9,9) is only shown to contain 9; HKRS list quadratic (3n², 6n²) and quartic (n⁴, 4n⁴) obstructions for it, so it is not unobstructed (v2.0.0 renamed rootUnobstructed -> rootSquareContrast).
- **Limit.** The mod-24 residue-set comparison is computational, not in Lean.
- **Limit.** The full Apollonian local-global problem remains open.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`
- **Source.** [apollonian-sandpile-markov](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov) `v3.0.0` · archive entry `0TH-2026-018` · [paper](papers/0TH-2026-018_apollonian-sandpile-markov/Donahue_2026_apollonian_sandpile_markov.pdf)

<a id="r76"></a>
### R76. Abelian sandpile toppling laws and small-grid Laplacian computations

- **Statement.** For symmetric zero-diagonal adjacency on integer configurations: topplings commute; any legal trace fires each vertex at most w times for a stabilizing odometer w; two legal stabilizing traces have equal counts and results. Via a zero-Mathlib n×n Laplace determinant detMat, Lean proves detMat 4 (gridLap 2) = 192, detMat 9 (gridLap 3) = 100352 (T2b), detMat 4 (a2GridLap 2) = 1092, detMat 9 (a2GridLap 3) = 5393920 (T2f), and legal Z² and A₂ Dhar traces.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`ApollonianSandpileMarkov.Palomar.t2a_abelian_sandpile_confluence_and_least_action`](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov/blob/94ece6a8dce9c9a30c6a2ada2eb335cb0501f770/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L35), [`ApollonianSandpileMarkov.Palomar.t2b_dhar_critical_identity_and_grid_orders`](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov/blob/94ece6a8dce9c9a30c6a2ada2eb335cb0501f770/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L38), [`ApollonianSandpileMarkov.Palomar.t2f_a2_grid_orders_and_dhar_identity`](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov/blob/94ece6a8dce9c9a30c6a2ada2eb335cb0501f770/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L50); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. V2.0.0 proves the 9×9 Z² and A₂ Dirichlet Laplacian determinants directly via zero-Mathlib detMat (replacing the v1.0.0 indirect similarity factorization); the Matrix-Tree / sandpile-group-order interpretation remains outside Lean.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Re-audit pending.** Audited at commit `94ece6a`; the archive pin has since moved to `bc6464b`.
- **Limit.** No sink condition and no termination of stabilization are formalized.
- **Limit.** The 4×4 and 9×9 determinants are proved directly in Lean via detMat (v2.0.0), but the Matrix-Tree theorem and sandpile-group-order identification are not formalized.
- **Limit.** The identity configurations are shown to be stabilizations from Dhar's formula, not proved to be the group identity.
- **Limit.** Abelian property (Dhar 1990) and least action principle are known.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`
- **Source.** [apollonian-sandpile-markov](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov) `v3.0.0` · archive entry `0TH-2026-018` · [paper](papers/0TH-2026-018_apollonian-sandpile-markov/Donahue_2026_apollonian_sandpile_markov.pdf)

<a id="r77"></a>
### R77. Fricke-Cohn trace identity and congruences of Markov numbers, including Zhang's mod-32 theorem

- **Statement.** For 2×2 integer matrices of determinant 1, tr(A)² + tr(B)² + tr(AB)² = tr A·tr B·tr AB + tr[A,B] + 2, and Cohn-tree nodes satisfy 3 | tr(A), 3 | tr(B), 3 | tr(AB) and give Markov triples via tr/3. For triples reachable from (1,1,1) by Vieta moves: pairwise coprime; odd prime divisors and odd entries are ≡ 1 (mod 4); even entries are ≡ 2 (mod 32), with 2 and 34 occurring.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`ApollonianSandpileMarkov.Palomar.t3a_sl2z_fricke_cohn_markov_correspondence`](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov/blob/94ece6a8dce9c9a30c6a2ada2eb335cb0501f770/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L53), [`ApollonianSandpileMarkov.Palomar.t3b_markov_coprimality_odd_primes_and_mod32`](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov/blob/94ece6a8dce9c9a30c6a2ada2eb335cb0501f770/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L56); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Mod-32 statement is Zhang, Acta Arith. 128 (2007), arXiv:math/0612620.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Re-audit pending.** Audited at commit `94ece6a`; the archive pin has since moved to `bc6464b`.
- **Limit.** The mod-32 theorem and its sharpness witnesses were published by Ying Zhang (Acta Arith. 128 (2007), arXiv:math/0612620); v2.0.0 attributes the theorem to Zhang and drops the v1.0.0 'our Exact Mod-32 Theorem' phrasing.
- **Limit.** Statements are for the first coordinate of tree triples; that every positive Markov triple lies in the tree (Markov's theorem) is not formalized.
- **Limit.** The Markov uniqueness conjecture (NT-083) remains open and is not addressed.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`

- **Statement.** For explicit subtraction-free integer polynomials P₁,…,P₅ in x₁, x₂, x₃: P₁P₄ = x₁⁴x₂²x₃²P₂² + P₃² and P₂P₅ = x₁⁶x₂⁴x₃²P₃² + P₄² identically; each Pᵢ ≥ 1 at positive integer inputs; at (1,1,1) the values are 2, 5, 29, 433, 37666.

<a id="r79"></a>
### R79. Admissible coefficients of the four-variable Markov-Hurwitz equation

- **Statement.** Positive integer solutions of x₁² + x₂² + x₃² + x₄² = a·x₁x₂x₃x₄ exist only for a = 1 (all xᵢ ≡ 2 mod 4) or a = 4 (all xᵢ odd); there are none for a ∈ {2, 3} or a ≥ 5. Explicit solutions are given for a = 1 and a = 4.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declaration [`ApollonianSandpileMarkov.Palomar.t3e_markov_hurwitz_n4_complete_classification`](https://github.com/0thernes/apollonian-sandpile-markov/blob/94ece6a8dce9c9a30c6a2ada2eb335cb0501f770/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L65); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.

- **Re-audit pending.** Audited at commit `94ece6a`; the archive pin has since moved to `bc6464b`.
- **Limit.** Classical (Hurwitz 1907, Arch. Math. Phys. 11, 185-196); v2.0.0 cites Hurwitz (1907).
- **Limit.** The description of all solutions as Vieta orbits is not part of the exported statement; v2.0.0 narrows the prose to admissible coefficient classification (keeping the Lean identifier name for comparator continuity).
- **Limit.** Hurwitz equations for n ≥ 5 are not addressed.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`

<a id="r80"></a>
### R80. Sedenion box-kite structure of the 42 assessors

- **Statement.** The 42 sedenion pairs (i, j), 1 ≤ i ≤ 7, 9 ≤ j ≤ 15, j ≠ i+8, with adjacency '(e_i+e_j)(e_k ± e_l) = 0 for some sign', form a 4-regular graph that splits into 7 components of 6 vertices, each 4-regular (an octahedron K_{2,2,2}); span{e_0,…,e_8} is norm-multiplicative.
- **Related source.** [R. de Marrais, The 42 Assessors and the Box-Kites They Fly (2000), arXiv:math/0011260](https://arxiv.org/abs/math/0011260), abstract (source of the known result, not an open question).
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`CDTerrace.Palomar.prove_t4b`](https://github.com/0thernes/cayley-dickson-terrace-frontier/blob/cbd146a3f174a39fe9cfb656d7fd6f93c5bd64be/lean/PalomarSolution.lean#L37), [`CDTerrace.sedenion_box_kites_7x6`](https://github.com/0thernes/cayley-dickson-terrace-frontier/blob/cbd146a3f174a39fe9cfb656d7fd6f93c5bd64be/lean/CDTerrace/FiniteCertificates.lean#L1776); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement matches the stated claim; prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** The 42 assessors and the 7 box-kites are due to de Marrais (arXiv:math/0011260, 2000); this is a formal six-kernel re-verification.
- **Limit.** In v2.0.0, inter-strut disjointness is proved directly from pure vanishing-product adjacency (isBoxKiteAssessor) linked to cdMulCoordGamma without XOR pre-filtering.
- **Limit.** Norm-multiplicativity of span{e_0,…,e_8} is checked via isNormMultMask 4 511 (cross-term cancellation over basis quadruples).
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`
- **Source.** [cayley-dickson-terrace-frontier](https://github.com/0thernes/cayley-dickson-terrace-frontier) `v2.0.0` · archive entry `0TH-2026-019` · [paper](papers/0TH-2026-019_cayley-dickson-terrace-frontier/Donahue_2026_cayley_dickson_terrace_frontier.pdf)

[`FormalAtlas.Challenge.thm_lonely_runner_n2`](https://github.com/0thernes/unsolved-mathematics/blob/0604df5c70c850df44f3fdd20519e46c82925224/lean/Solution.lean#L126), Perfect-number items use a computational divSum whose equality with σ(n) is checked only in pytest; finite checks by kernel decide.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** No open conjecture is proven: Collatz, Erdős–Straus, Lonely Runner, Brocard, twin primes, Goldbach, odd perfect numbers and Mersenne infinitude all remain open.
- **Limit.** All headline results are classical or elementary. The Lonely Runner conjecture is proved for up to 13 runners in recent preprints (Rosenfeld, arXiv:2509.14111; Trakulthongchai, arXiv:2511.22427; Sungkawichai and Trakulthongchai, arXiv:2604.23906), far beyond the n ≤ 2 cases and the t = 1/4 subfamily of n = 3 formalized here.
- **Limit.** The n = 3 Lonely Runner item covers 27 of 64 speed-triple classes mod 4, not 7/8 as the prose states.
- **Limit.** The 'ε-invariance barrier' is formalized only as facts about the 3n−1 map; the claim that ε-symmetric arguments cannot settle Collatz is informal.
- **Limit.** Six-kernel replay and lake comparator were not re-run locally; evidence is CI run 45742865067 at the pinned commit.
- **Limit.** Also kernel-checked: 2^n − 1 prime implies n prime (thm_mersenne_exponent_prime).
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin (latest release `v0.8.2`). They are not covered by this audit.

<a id="section-f"></a>
## F. Engineering invariant suites

*Machine-checked properties of a software model or simulator. They say the model is internally consistent, not that it describes nature.*

<a id="r43"></a>
### R43. Conservation and monotonicity facts for the repository's discrete vesicle, ATP and SNARE models

- **Statement.** For the repository's definitions: every valid vesicle-pool step list keeps rrp+recycling+reserve; if s<b+f·c, the store (a₀+s·t)∸(b+f·c)·t is below a₀ when a₀,t>0 and is 0 for t≥⌊a₀/D⌋+1, D=b+f·c−s; if b,c>0 and b+f·c≤s, then f·c<s, (s−b)·c<s·c, and ⌊(s−b)/c⌋<⌊s/c⌋ when c≤b; (8n)/8=n; the clamped SNARE barrier is non-increasing in Ca²⁺ and SNARE counts.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`SynapseBouton.vesicle_pool_transition_invariant`](https://github.com/0thernes/human-synapse-bouton-lab/blob/06e0dcdd5fc4cb406e9fe21d260ff94b68a414d4/lean/Solution.lean#L297), [`SynapseBouton.vesicle_pool_multistep_invariant`](https://github.com/0thernes/human-synapse-bouton-lab/blob/06e0dcdd5fc4cb406e9fe21d260ff94b68a414d4/lean/Solution.lean#L306), [`SynapseBouton.atp_deficit_strict_depletion`](https://github.com/0thernes/human-synapse-bouton-lab/blob/06e0dcdd5fc4cb406e9fe21d260ff94b68a414d4/lean/Solution.lean#L319), [`SynapseBouton.atp_hitting_time_upper_bound`](https://github.com/0thernes/human-synapse-bouton-lab/blob/06e0dcdd5fc4cb406e9fe21d260ff94b68a414d4/lean/Solution.lean#L327), [`SynapseBouton.resting_leak_strictly_lowers_sustainable_rate`](https://github.com/0thernes/human-synapse-bouton-lab/blob/06e0dcdd5fc4cb406e9fe21d260ff94b68a414d4/lean/Solution.lean#L349), [`SynapseBouton.kinesin_processive_atp_exact`](https://github.com/0thernes/human-synapse-bouton-lab/blob/06e0dcdd5fc4cb406e9fe21d260ff94b68a414d4/lean/Solution.lean#L364), [`SynapseBouton.snare_barrier_monotone_in_syt1_ca`](https://github.com/0thernes/human-synapse-bouton-lab/blob/06e0dcdd5fc4cb406e9fe21d260ff94b68a414d4/lean/Solution.lean#L374), [`SynapseBouton.snare_copy_number_cooperativity`](https://github.com/0thernes/human-synapse-bouton-lab/blob/06e0dcdd5fc4cb406e9fe21d260ff94b68a414d4/lean/Solution.lean#L393); axioms: propext, Quot.sound. True by construction of self-defined update rules; the H3 simulation outcome itself rests on Rust code outside Lean.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** T15 is an upper bound on the zero time, not an exact hitting time; it equals 3 s only for the deterministic mean-rate H3 constants, while the simulator jitters the rate each second.
- **Limit.** The ATP and SNARE constants are the author's and are not found in the cited sources; the Lean definitions are hand-written analogues of the TypeScript/Rust engines with no tested link.
- **Limit.** T17 contains no kinesin model; the SNARE-barrier claims of T18 and T19 are non-strict (T19's only strict part is the zipper energy 115·n₁ < 115·n₂).
- **Limit.** No part of NEURO-001 (consciousness) or NEURO-019 (20 W credit assignment) is addressed.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`

<a id="r83"></a>
### R83. Lean 4 model invariants for the PLANARIA .hbin erasure-coded executable format

- **Statement.** In the Lean model: GF(2^8) arithmetic mod 0x11D forms a field; the 2×2 Cauchy code decodes exactly for distinct coordinates ≥ 2 (k = 2 only); the fixed schedule gives each half ≥ k+2 cells per bundle; a burst of ≤ 16 bytes touches ≤ 8 even and ≤ 8 odd positions; a quorum with two tied maximal buckets of count ≥ 2 returns none; RAM masking stays below 4096; CNOT preserves the squared norm.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Planaria.GF256.gf256_cauchy2_exact_decode`](https://github.com/0thernes/planaria/blob/6979cc1be4eaaf8d6af30387fe1ace3ec7586d00/lean/PlanariaProofs/GF256.lean#L639), [`Planaria.Mitosis.planaria_schedule_nodup_and_sorted_halves`](https://github.com/0thernes/planaria/blob/6979cc1be4eaaf8d6af30387fe1ace3ec7586d00/lean/PlanariaProofs/MitosisBounds.lean#L156), [`Planaria.Byzantine.interleaved_burst_16_even_odd_bound`](https://github.com/0thernes/planaria/blob/6979cc1be4eaaf8d6af30387fe1ace3ec7586d00/lean/PlanariaProofs/ByzantineImmune.lean#L151), [`Planaria.Byzantine.unpinned_quorum_tie_fail_closed`](https://github.com/0thernes/planaria/blob/6979cc1be4eaaf8d6af30387fe1ace3ec7586d00/lean/PlanariaProofs/ByzantineImmune.lean#L462), [`Planaria.Xenograft.pvm32_ram_bounds_invariant`](https://github.com/0thernes/planaria/blob/6979cc1be4eaaf8d6af30387fe1ace3ec7586d00/lean/PlanariaProofs/XenograftABI.lean#L24), [`Planaria.QuantumTemporal.cnot4Q_preserves_normSq4`](https://github.com/0thernes/planaria/blob/6979cc1be4eaaf8d6af30387fe1ace3ec7586d00/lean/PlanariaProofs/QuantumTemporal.lean#L150), [`Planaria.GF256.gf256Field`](https://github.com/0thernes/planaria/blob/6979cc1be4eaaf8d6af30387fe1ace3ec7586d00/lean/PlanariaProofs/GF256.lean#L462), [`Planaria.Mitosis.planaria_schedule_exact_halves`](https://github.com/0thernes/planaria/blob/6979cc1be4eaaf8d6af30387fe1ace3ec7586d00/lean/PlanariaProofs/MitosisBounds.lean#L135), [`Planaria.QuantumTemporal.hadamardScaled4Q_normSq4`](https://github.com/0thernes/planaria/blob/6979cc1be4eaaf8d6af30387fe1ace3ec7586d00/lean/PlanariaProofs/QuantumTemporal.lean#L88); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Concerns a Lean model, not extracted code; RS decoding, k>2 recovery and benchmarks are tested outside Lean.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** Cauchy decoding is proved only for k = 2; bundles with k in {4, 6, 8, 16} rely on the classical Cauchy determinant theorem and tests.
- **Limit.** No Reed-Solomon code or decoder is formalized; the 8-errors-per-lane radius is arithmetic on 254 − 238 + 1.
- **Limit.** The Merkle quarantine theorem assumes an authenticity-root correspondence (HashBinding) rather than deriving it from a hash.
- **Limit.** The JavaScript runtime, Python oracle and survival benchmarks lie outside Lean.
- **Limit.** The field laws, Cauchy inversion and counting bounds are textbook mathematics; no open problem is addressed.
- **Limit.** The quorum result also needs every other bucket to be at most the tied count; lanes are modelled as even and odd byte positions.
- **Limit.** The scaled Hadamard doubles the squared norm (hadamardScaled4Q_normSq4), so 'integer-amplitude gates preserve the squared norm' holds for CNOT, not for H.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.

- **Limit.** The 202 audited declarations decompose into 127 core theorems/corollaries, 56 zero-Mathlib helper lemmas, 7 adversarial refutations, and 12 isolated comparator target bindings across 13 Palomar module files all <1,000 lines.
- **Limit.** Six-kernel lake comparator --paranoid checks ran on Ubuntu CI run 46014442260 (bubblewrap) covering all 12 isolated comparator targets and their dependency closure, alongside live negative tamper-rejection (scripts/test_lean_negative.py --with-comparator); full-library compilation was checked by lake build, leanchecker --fresh, and lake check --paranoid.
- **Limit.** Both btz_primal_feasible and gen_bridge_primal_sub_optimal / gen_bridge_primal_sup_optimal prove plan non-negativity and marginal feasibility in Lean for the 3-class reductions, while FiniteGraphGaussBonnet.lean directly sums curvedGenKappaQ over arbitrary finite weighted edge lists.
- **Limit.** Strong subadditivity uses Nat edge weights and Bool vertex sets and takes S(ABC) and S(B) as hypothesised lower bounds over homologous cuts.

- **Limit.** Six-kernel replay is CI-only (Linux bubblewrap) and was not re-run in this audit.
- **Limit.** Bounds need the stated input ranges, for example CSS ≤ 10000 when difficulty, centrality and age are ≤ 100, and Titan in [90, 1000] when difficulty, impact and interdisciplinarity are in [1, 10] and the degree term is ≤ 100.
- **Limit.** The 7-layer list has a size theorem only, and the era map has monotonicity and samples but no iff characterisation.
- **Limit.** AQS has a bound but is shown monotone in only three of its five inputs, so AQS monotonicity is not claimed.
- **Limit.** The handshaking lemma needs a duplicate-free vertex list that contains every edge endpoint.

<a id="r86"></a>
### R86. Discrete invariant suite for the Cellular Engine Omega software model

- **Statement.** Lean 4.35.0 lemmas over Nat, Int and List about the engine's discrete software model: commutation of pointwise updates at distinct indices of a Nat→Int map and list-sum conservation under local steps, an integer projection that zeroes a list sum, 2×2 integer Jacobian sign algebra, Nat power inequalities, closed arithmetic checks on hard-coded constants, and equalities among C11-exported receipt literals (O63, O72); 100 audited items.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`OmegaCellular.BioInteractionNets.O42_active_pair_diamond_strong_confluence`](https://github.com/0thernes/cellular-engine-omega/blob/4b257af5f2659ab29a29dc026a17d1c92d011ace/lean/OmegaCellularProofs/BioInteractionNets.lean#L31), [`OmegaCellular.VirtualCellManifold.O56_virtual_cell_physics_projection_conservation`](https://github.com/0thernes/cellular-engine-omega/blob/4b257af5f2659ab29a29dc026a17d1c92d011ace/lean/OmegaCellularProofs/VirtualCellManifold.lean#L45), [`OmegaCellular.EigenProofreadingInfo.O06_hopfield_ninio_proofreading_beats_equilibrium`](https://github.com/0thernes/cellular-engine-omega/blob/4b257af5f2659ab29a29dc026a17d1c92d011ace/lean/OmegaCellularProofs/EigenProofreadingInfo.lean#L18), [`OmegaCellular.TuringFeinbergCRNT.O12_turing_equal_diffusion_impossibility`](https://github.com/0thernes/cellular-engine-omega/blob/4b257af5f2659ab29a29dc026a17d1c92d011ace/lean/OmegaCellularProofs/TuringFeinbergCRNT.lean#L21), [`OmegaCellular.VerifyTraceReceipt.O63_live_c11_receipt_verified`](https://github.com/0thernes/cellular-engine-omega/blob/4b257af5f2659ab29a29dc026a17d1c92d011ace/lean/OmegaCellularProofs/VerifyTraceReceipt.lean#L90), [`OmegaCellular.SubstratesCube.O72_live_c11_cube_receipt_verified`](https://github.com/0thernes/cellular-engine-omega/blob/4b257af5f2659ab29a29dc026a17d1c92d011ace/lean/OmegaCellularProofs/SubstratesCube.lean#L34); axioms: propext, Quot.sound. Receipt values come from one C11 run outside Lean; floating-point solvers and C memory safety are tested empirically, not proved.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: not yet searched.
- **Limit.** The lemmas concern the integer software model; no biological or continuum-physics law (Poincaré–Hopf, Turing instability, TUR) is formalized in general.
- **Limit.** At least 16 of the 100 items are closed arithmetic checks on literals, for example 661 + 274 + 20 + 45 = 1000.
- **Limit.** O63 and O72 check equalities among literals written by one C11 run at seed 2026001; no hash is verified in Lean.
- **Limit.** Independent-checker and comparator runs were confirmed only through CI run 45810663954, not reproduced locally.
- **Limit.** Solves no open problem; the repository states 0/1,900 Mega-Atlas problems retired.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`
- **Source.** [cellular-engine-omega](https://github.com/0thernes/cellular-engine-omega) `v5.3.0` · archive entry `0TH-BUILD-008` · [paper](builds/0TH-BUILD-008_cellular-engine-omega/Donahue_2026_cellular_engine_omega.pdf)

<a id="r87"></a>
### R87. Kernel-checked finite-field and bitvector invariants of the AUTOPHAGE engine model

- **Statement.** For the repository's Lean models: under a trial-division irreducibility test (divisors of degree 1–4), exactly 30 of the 256 polynomials x⁸+p over F₂ pass, and they match the 30-entry table in src/galois_math.c; for each, the 2×2 Cauchy code [[1,3],[5,1]] recovers both data bytes from the two parity bytes; affine GF(2) maps with linear inverses round-trip; Fredkin conserves popcount; masked-XOR kinship is transitive; multi-step integer ledgers keep a zero residual.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`Autophage.T12a_full_trial_div_matches_c11_table`](https://github.com/0thernes/autophage/blob/1956c2cc54845ebadc54b2a5276cbc66bf031a8b/lean/AutophageProofs.lean#L614), [`Autophage.T16_planarian_50pct_exact_recovery`](https://github.com/0thernes/autophage/blob/1956c2cc54845ebadc54b2a5276cbc66bf031a8b/lean/AutophageProofs.lean#L771), [`Autophage.T05_weismann_exact_roundtrip`](https://github.com/0thernes/autophage/blob/1956c2cc54845ebadc54b2a5276cbc66bf031a8b/lean/AutophageProofs.lean#L172), [`Autophage.T20a_fredkin64_popcnt_conserved`](https://github.com/0thernes/autophage/blob/1956c2cc54845ebadc54b2a5276cbc66bf031a8b/lean/AutophageProofs.lean#L876), [`Autophage.T25_ultrametric_strong_triangle`](https://github.com/0thernes/autophage/blob/1956c2cc54845ebadc54b2a5276cbc66bf031a8b/lean/AutophageProofs.lean#L907), [`Autophage.T29a_first_law_ledger_multistep`](https://github.com/0thernes/autophage/blob/1956c2cc54845ebadc54b2a5276cbc66bf031a8b/lean/AutophageProofs.lean#L984); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Facts about Lean definitions, not the emitted x86-64 code; several of the 48 theorems are one-line arithmetic identities.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Limit.** The theorems are about Lean models; JIT machine-code emission, C11 memory safety and FP64 drift are checked only by tests.
- **Limit.** T21, T28, T29 and T30 are tautologies (for example sink ≤ sink + rad) despite Landauer, First-Law and Second-Law names.
- **Limit.** The count of 30 irreducible binary octics and the reversible-gate identities are textbook results.
- **Limit.** No open problem, including AI-045 or BIO-001, is resolved; biological terms are metaphors (LIMITATIONS.md sec. 1).
- **Limit.** Six-kernel acceptance was confirmed from the CI log only; replay needs Linux bubblewrap.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean VerifyAxioms.lean`
- **Source.** [autophage](https://github.com/0thernes/autophage) `v1.4.0` · archive entry `0TH-BUILD-009` · [paper](builds/0TH-BUILD-009_autophage/Donahue_2026_autophage.pdf)

<a id="r88"></a>
### R88. Lean invariants for a ten-pool integer energy-ledger model and helper encodings

- **Statement.** In the Lean model of a ten-field unbounded-integer ledger: guarded ATP→heat, patch→gut and ATP→tissue transfers, the equal-amount pair ATP→neural then neural→heat, and the composite tick built from them leave the field sum unchanged; ATP→heat never lowers the heat field and preserves non-negativity of all fields. Also: a region<4 edit guard returns its input, 2-bit ternary encoding round-trips on {−1,0,1}, 2·(1+2+…+n)=n(n+1), 16·2^Q≤16384 for Q≤10, and hard-coded receipt constants satisfy stated equalities and bounds.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`NoeticProofs.N01_atp_to_heat_conserves_sum`](https://github.com/0thernes/noetic-organism-omega/blob/c4301d926fa12334f8f8f54b2994dfc12ff649b8/lean/NoeticProofs.lean#L20), [`NoeticProofs.N09_closed_tick_exact_conservation`](https://github.com/0thernes/noetic-organism-omega/blob/c4301d926fa12334f8f8f54b2994dfc12ff649b8/lean/NoeticProofs.lean#L89), [`NoeticProofs.N02_atp_to_heat_monotone_sink`](https://github.com/0thernes/noetic-organism-omega/blob/c4301d926fa12334f8f8f54b2994dfc12ff649b8/lean/NoeticProofs.lean#L26), [`NoeticProofs.N25_ring0_acl_blocks_protected_regions`](https://github.com/0thernes/noetic-organism-omega/blob/c4301d926fa12334f8f8f54b2994dfc12ff649b8/lean/NoeticProofs.lean#L203), [`NoeticProofs.N33_quantum_cap_10_bytes_le_16384`](https://github.com/0thernes/noetic-organism-omega/blob/c4301d926fa12334f8f8f54b2994dfc12ff649b8/lean/NoeticProofs.lean#L257), [`NoeticProofs.N24_live_c11_receipt_verified`](https://github.com/0thernes/noetic-organism-omega/blob/c4301d926fa12334f8f8f54b2994dfc12ff649b8/lean/NoeticProofs.lean#L187); axioms: propext, Quot.sound (N01, N03, N09, N15, N33); none (N24, N25, N29); Classical.choice appears only in N22. Statements are about Lean definitions in lean/NoeticProofs/Spec.lean; the C11 engine is linked only by constants printed by export-lean.
- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: not yet searched.
- **Limit.** Lean's Ledger10 uses unbounded Int with 10 fields; the C11 ledger is int64_t with 11 slots, and no theorem covers overflow or the C source.
- **Limit.** 18 of the 54 theorems are closed numeral identities (for example N41: 825 - 5 = 820) and certify nothing about the engine.
- **Limit.** N24, N32 and N48 only check, by decide, constants printed by one C11 run; CI regenerates that file on Linux, where the world-state SHA-256 differs from the committed Windows receipt.
- **Limit.** N25 'Ring-0 ACL' is a one-line if-expression in Lean, not a hardware or C11 access-control proof; N02/N06 'Second-Law' are field monotonicity under a non-negative guard.
- **Limit.** No consciousness, cognition, biology or physics result is shown; 29 cognitive checks are indicator-only by the repository's own statement.
- **Limit.** ATP→neural and neural→heat are proved to conserve the sum only as a pair with the same amount, not as separate public theorems.
- **Limit.** The added N02, N04, N05 and N07 depend on propext and Quot.sound, like N01.

<a id="r89"></a>
### R89. Arithmetic conservation and addressing checks for an abstract model of an artificial-life engine

- **Statement.** In the engine's Lean model over ℕ: moving dm≤bioMass from biomass to cell mass, or de≤bioEnergy to heat, preserves the respective total; for cur≤cap a capped receive splits q into kept+rejected=q with cur+kept≤cap; six face fluxes each ≤amt/8 sum to ≤amt; converting k≤nutXMass weight-24 units into weight-16 biomass plus thermal energy preserves weighted energy; for uid<2^32, tkey0 xor (uid/2^32)=tkey0, but not universally; offsets 8·n are injective and below 32 GiB for n<2^32.
- **Evidence.** Lean kernel; declarations [`AsmodensiusProofs.A01_bio_to_cell_mass_conserved`](https://github.com/0thernes/asmodensius-native/blob/67bedd4abb9d03c7b94e70c8bf46f5b5f6893e6d/lean/AsmodensiusProofs/Core.lean#L26), [`AsmodensiusProofs.A02_energy_dissipation_conserved`](https://github.com/0thernes/asmodensius-native/blob/67bedd4abb9d03c7b94e70c8bf46f5b5f6893e6d/lean/AsmodensiusProofs/Core.lean#L32), [`AsmodensiusProofs.A21_receive_cap_mass_conserved`](https://github.com/0thernes/asmodensius-native/blob/67bedd4abb9d03c7b94e70c8bf46f5b5f6893e6d/lean/AsmodensiusProofs/Core.lean#L117), [`AsmodensiusProofs.A22_six_face_flux_never_overdraws`](https://github.com/0thernes/asmodensius-native/blob/67bedd4abb9d03c7b94e70c8bf46f5b5f6893e6d/lean/AsmodensiusProofs/Core.lean#L124), [`AsmodensiusProofs.A23_nut_x_to_bio_energy_conserved`](https://github.com/0thernes/asmodensius-native/blob/67bedd4abb9d03c7b94e70c8bf46f5b5f6893e6d/lean/AsmodensiusProofs/Core.lean#L132), [`AsmodensiusProofs.A17_bulk_philox_key_equivalence`](https://github.com/0thernes/asmodensius-native/blob/67bedd4abb9d03c7b94e70c8bf46f5b5f6893e6d/lean/AsmodensiusProofs/Core.lean#L96), [`AsmodensiusProofs.A30_refute_unbounded_philox_bulk_key_eq`](https://github.com/0thernes/asmodensius-native/blob/67bedd4abb9d03c7b94e70c8bf46f5b5f6893e6d/lean/AsmodensiusProofs/NonVacuity.lean#L50), [`AsmodensiusProofs.A26_gpu_offset8_injective_and_32gib_range`](https://github.com/0thernes/asmodensius-native/blob/67bedd4abb9d03c7b94e70c8bf46f5b5f6893e6d/lean/AsmodensiusProofs/Core.lean#L150); axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. ### R93. Integer ledger and carbon-nitrogen trace conservation and capped-simplex bounds for an artificial-life model

- **Statement.** In the ALife software's integer Lean model: finite transfer/import/export traces leave the 11-pool residual total−(initial+imports−exports) unchanged; finite assimilate/shed/mineralize traces conserve total C and total N; each assimilation step changes biomass C by exactly 4 times its biomass N change; any 7 natural weights summing to 10000, each ≤4800, are all <5000 with at least 3 nonzero; plus Pythagoras for orthogonal equal-length integer lists, 2p³<p⁴ for p≥3, and arithmetic on hard-coded constants.

- **Limit.** The six-kernel acceptance was seen in CI run 45824894491 but not re-run locally (it needs Linux bubblewrap).
- **Limit.** Ledger transfers are guarded only by pool indices and a non-negative amount, so donor pools may become negative.
- **Limit.** The 4:1 ratio is a single-step fact that holds trivially when the assimilation guard fails; shed and mineralize steps move C and N independently.
- **Upstream.** The source repository has commits newer than the archived pin. They are not covered by this audit.
- **Check it.** `cd lean && lake build && lake env lean ALifeProofs/VerifyAxioms.lean`
- **Source.** [ALife-Framework](https://github.com/0thernes/ALife-Framework) `v3.0.0` · archive entry `0TH-BUILD-016` · [paper](builds/0TH-BUILD-016_ALife-Framework/Donahue_2026_alife_framework_v3.pdf)

### R94. Lean invariants for the BEAST bare-metal AI software model

- **Statement.** In the BEAST software's Lean model: literal arithmetic on constants named after FIPS-203/197, Multiboot1/2 checksums and the GGUF Q4_0 block; bookkeeping sums (DLL counts, planted-mutant totals, matrix and MoE splits); five base-3 digits pack to a value below 243 that unpacks to the same digits; ceil-division alignment bounds; BitVec XOR identities; commutativity of dual-number addition and multiplication; small Bool/Nat models of rewrite, spend-cap and loopback rules; and equalities and bounds on hard-coded receipt constants.

- **Audit (2026-11-26).** Rebuilt from a clean clone at the audited commit: pass; the formal statement is narrower than the usual informal claim (see Limits); prior work: known mathematics.
- **Re-audit pending.** Audited at commit `c421135`; the archive pin has since moved to `ccb67a8`.
- **Limit.** The Lean definitions are standalone models and are not connected to the Zig, Rust, C or PTX code that ships.
- **Limit.** B60 and B65 check constants committed in LiveReceipt.lean; they do not show that any run produced them.
- **Limit.** B54 proves only commutativity of dual-number multiplication, not the product rule its docstring names.
- **Limit.** Timings, GPU kernels, DLL results and QEMU boots are empirical and outside the Lean kernel.
- **Limit.** No open problem is resolved; the repository states 0 of 1,900 Mega-Atlas problems retired.
- **Limit.** Of the 65 theorems, 39 are closed literal statements, including every spec-constant, bookkeeping and receipt clause listed here.
- **Limit.** The pack/unpack theorem gives a left inverse on all 243 digit tuples; surjectivity onto 0 to 242 is not stated.

## Not claimed

- No Millennium Prize problem and no grand open problem is solved here. P versus NP is open.
- No result has been refereed by a journal or checked by a named outside expert.
- Engineering invariant suites (section F) verify software models. They are not evidence about consciousness, sentience, biology or physics.
- A search that found no earlier proof is not a proof of novelty.

## How a result gets on this list

1. The claim is registered in the archive with a pinned commit and a frozen paper.
2. The formal statement is read against the informal claim and against the cited source, in a session that did not write the proof.
3. The proof is rebuilt from a clean clone at that commit, or the ledger says it was not.
4. The literature is searched for earlier or parallel proofs and the outcome is recorded.
5. The wording is limited to what steps 2 to 4 support. Anything narrower than the usual informal claim is stated under Limit.

## How to challenge a result

- Open an issue on this repository or on the result's repository and quote the result number (for example R7).
- A reproducible counterexample, a gap in a statement audit, or an earlier proof in the literature leads to a correction: the result is narrowed, reclassified or withdrawn, and keeps its number.
- Corrections are recorded in CHANGELOG.md.
